Cubre 360→1440, touch targets, texto ilegible y grids que no apilan. ([yojananyosef/logos](https://github.com/yojananyosef/logos/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skill/responsive-audit))
- **responsive-audit-inline-skill**: In-session responsive audit delegate — wraps the playwright-responsive-audit-skill inline loop (detect → fix → re-verify) for current-diff frontend changes. Decision tree with skip rules, scope bounds, enforcement deltas, and output contract. Triggers: inline responsive audit, viewport check in-session, plan-execution-inline-skill responsive step. ([darellchua2/civiltekk-skills](https://github.com/darellchua2/civiltekk-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/responsive-audit-inline-skill) · ★7)
- **responsive-authoring**: Express responsive behavior with ResponsiveJS (r$) instead of media queries — fluid values, geometry predicates, container-bound components. Use when writing or refactoring responsive CSS/JS, when a breakpoint number is being chosen, or when a layout must react to content it cannot know in advance. ([AleSaiani/ResponsiveJS](https://github.com/AleSaiani/ResponsiveJS/tree/HEAD/plugins/responsivejs/skills/responsive-authoring) · ★1)
- **responsive-behavior-planner**: Defines how layout, controls, content priority, and interactions adapt across available space. Use when the user asks to plan responsive behavior, breakpoint strategy, container behavior, mobile/tablet/desktop adaptations, navigation changes, content reflow, or interaction differences before implementation. ([vmitsaras/Agent-Skills](https://github.com/vmitsaras/Agent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ui-ux-polish/responsive-behavior-planner))
- **responsive-block-patterns**: How every block and component transforms across breakpoints: container queries, fluid scaling, breakpoint transformation catalogs, responsive grids, and mobile-first CSS with production code. Use when a layout must survive small screens, or when specifying responsive behavior for handoff. ([phazurlabs/sumi](https://github.com/phazurlabs/sumi/tree/HEAD/skills/responsive-block-patterns) · ★59 · 5 copies)
